
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `station` (
`s_id` int(20) NOT NULL,
  `acct_no` int(6) NOT NULL,
  `sname` varchar(60) NOT NULL,
  `saddress` varchar(80) NOT NULL,
  `scity` varchar(80) NOT NULL,
  `default_depot` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `slatitude` float(10,6) NOT NULL,
  `slongitude` float(10,6) NOT NULL,
  `stype` varchar(60) NOT NULL,
  `sproducts` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `petrol_nf` varchar(10) NOT NULL,
  `diesel_nf` varchar(10) NOT NULL,
  `stfhours` varchar(10) NOT NULL,
  `pno` varchar(10) NOT NULL,
  `sdistance` float NOT NULL,
  `master_route_id` int(6) NOT NULL,
  `master_route_name` varchar(30) DEFAULT NULL,
  `area` varchar(10) NOT NULL,
  `flood` int(11) NOT NULL,
  `LAD_status` varchar(8) DEFAULT NULL,
  `LSD_status` varchar(8) DEFAULT NULL,
  `LK_status` varchar(8) DEFAULT NULL,
  `95Petrol_status` varchar(8) DEFAULT NULL,
  `92Petrol_status` varchar(8) DEFAULT NULL,
  `contact` int(11) DEFAULT '711111111',
  `openhours` int(11) DEFAULT '24',
  `air` varchar(8) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'no',
  `bathroom` varchar(8) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'no',
  `atm` varchar(8) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'no',
  `market` varchar(8) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'no'
) ENGINE=MyISAM  DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 AUTO_INCREMENT=3020 ;
